## developer

<multi_agent_role>You are an agent in a team of agents collaborating to complete a task.

You can spawn sub-agents to handle subtasks, and those sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents. All agents in the team, including the agents that you can assign tasks to, are equally intelligent and capable, and have access to the same set of tools.

You can use `spawn_agent` to create a new agent, `followup_task` to give an existing agent a new task and trigger a turn, and `send_message` to pass a message to a running agent.
`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.

When you provide a response in the final channel, that content is immediately delivered back to your parent agent.
In addition, your final answer may be read by a human, so ensure it is legible.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: NEW_TASK | MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>
Payload:
<payload text>
```
You may also see them addressed as to=/root/..., which indicates your identity is /root/...

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Call `spawn_agent`, `send_message`, `followup_task`, `wait_agent`, `interrupt_agent`, and `list_agents` only as direct tool calls using the recipient shown in their tool definitions, such as `to=functions.collaboration.spawn_agent`, since they are intentionally absent from the `functions.exec` `tools.*` namespace. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. Only set `model` or `reasoning_effort` when explicitly requested by the user, applicable `AGENTS.md` instructions, or skill instructions; when doing so, set `fork_turns` to `"none"` or a positive integer string.</multi_agent_role>

  ],\r\n  },\r\n};\r\n\r\nconst DRILL_TABS: Tab[] = [\r\n  { label: "Drills", path: "/practice/drills", flags: ["has_drill_access"] },\r\n  { label: "Saved sets", path: "/practice/drills/saved", "Checking access" : `${tab.label} is locked for your account`}>\r\n        {tab.label}{permissions.loading ? null : <Lock size={13} aria-hidden="true" />}\r\n      </span>\r\n    );\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="student-destination">\r\n      <header className="student-destination-heading">\r\n        <div>\r\n          <h1>{config.title}</h1>\r\n          <p>{config.introduction}</p>\r\n        </div>\r\n      </header>\r\n      <nav className="student-destination-tabs" aria-label={`${config.title} sections`} aria-busy={permissions.loading}>\r\n        {renderTabs(config.tabs)}\r\n      </nav>\r\n      {destination === "practice" ? (\r\n        <nav className="student-destination-subtabs" aria-label={pathname.startsWith("/practice/exams") ? "Practice Exams pages" : "Drill pages"} aria-busy={permissions.loading}>\r\n          {renderTabs(pathname.startsWith("/practice/exams") ? EXAM_TABS : DRILL_TABS, true)}\r\n        </nav>\r\n      ) : null}\r\n      <div className="student-destination-content"><Outlet /></div>\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function DestinationLinks({\r\n  heading,\r\n  description,\r\n  links,\r\n}: {\r\n  heading: string;\r\n  description: string;\r\n  links: { label: string; description: string; path: string; flag?: PermissionFlag }[];\r\n}) {\r\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\r\n  return (\r\n    <section className="student-destination-links">\r\n      <h2>{heading}</h2>\r\n      <p>{description}</p>\r\n      <div className="student-destination-link-grid">\r\n        {links.map((link) => {\r\n          const available = !link.flag || (!permissions.loading && (permissions.is_admin || permissions[link.flag]));\r\n          const content = <><strong>{link.label}</strong><span>{link.description}</span><span aria-hidden="true">{available ? "Open ↗" : "Locked"}</span></>;\r\n          return available\r\n            ? <Link to={link.path} key={link.path} className="student-destination-link-card">{content}</Link>\r\n            : <div key={link.path} className="student-destination-link-card student-destination-link-locked" aria-disabled="true">{content}</div>;\r\n        })}\r\n      </div>\r\n    </section>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n'}]

They do different jobs, and the stimulus comes first because it gives you the material the rest of the question is testing.\\r\\n        </p>\\r\\n        <div className=\\"space-y-4\\">\\r\\n          <RowCard\\r\\n            left=\\"1. Stimulus\\"\\r\\n            right=\\"The short passage. It may be an argument, a set of facts, a debate, or a description. Your first job is simply to understand what it says.\\"\\r\\n          />\\r\\n          <RowCard\\r\\n            left=\\"2. Question Stem\\"\\r\\n            right=\\"The stem tells you what task to perform with the stimulus after you understand it.\\"\\r\\n          />\\r\\n          <RowCard\\r\\n            left=\\"3. Answer Choices\\"\\r\\n            right=\\"The choices test whether you understood the stimulus and can do the task the stem asks for. They do not replace close reading.\\"\\r\\n          />\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      </LessonSection>\\r\\n\\r\\n      <LessonSection tocTitle=\\"Reading Style\\" className=\\"space-y-8\\">\\r\\n        <SectionHeading title=\\"Reading Style\\" subtitle=\\"Passive reading vs. active reading\\" />\\r\\n        <div className=\\"grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2\\">\\r\\n          <GridCard title=\\"Passive Reading\\">\\r\\n            <p>Eyes move across the words, but the structure stays blurry.</p>\\r\\n            <p>You finish the passage and only have a vague impression of what happened.</p>\\r\\n            <p>That is when answer choices start to feel like guesses.</p>\\r\\n          </GridCard>\\r\\n          <GridCard title=\\"Active Reading\\">\\r\\n            <p>You track what each sentence is contributing.</p>\\r\\n            <p>You notice whether the passage is just information or whether it is trying to persuade you of something.</p>\\r\\n            <p>You reach the stem already knowing what the stimulus said.</p>\\r\\n          </GridCard>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n        <CalloutCard className=\\"bg-white\\">\\r\\n          <Kicker>Student job</Kicker>\\r\\n          <p className=\\"text-[20px] leading-[1.75] text-[rgba(26,26,26,0.84)]\\">\\r\\n            Stay with the passage long enough to classify it: is this merely information, or is this an argument?\\r\\n          </p>\\r\\n        </CalloutCard>\\r\\n      </LessonSection>\\r\\n\\r\\n      <LessonSection tocTitle=\\"Stimulus Example\\" className=\\"space-y-8\\">\\r\\n        <SectionHeading title=\\"Stimulus Example\\" subtitle=\\"Ask what the passage is saying\\" />\\r\\n        <CalloutCard className=\\"bg-[#FCFAF5]\\">\\r\\n          <p className=\\"font-serif text-[22px] italic leading-[1.7] text-[#1a1a1a]\\">\\r\\n            &quot;Last year, the city planted hundreds of new trees along its busiest streets. </div>\\r\\n);\\r\\n\\r\\nconst RuleList = ({ items }: { items: string[] }) => (\\r\\n  <ul className=\\"list-disc space-y-3 pl-5 text-[18px] leading-[1.68] text-[rgba(26,26,26,0.82)]\\">\\r\\n <IntroNotesShell\\r\\n      eyebrow=\\"Lesson 2\\"\\r\\n      title=\\"Argument Anatomy.\\"\\r\\n      subtitle=\\"Now that you can read the stimulus first, learn how to tell when it is making a case.\\"\\r\\n    >\\r\\n      <LessonSection tocTitle=\\"What an argument is\\" className=\\"space-y-8\\">\\r\\n        <SectionHeading title=\\"What an argument is\\" subtitle=\\"A claim supported by reasons\\" />\\r\\n        <p className=\\"font-serif text-[30px] italic leading-[1.35] text-[#1a1a1a]\\">\\r\\n          An argument is not just a topic. It is a claim the author wants you to accept, backed by reasons the author offers in support.\\r\\n        </p>\\r\\n        <CalloutCard>\\r\\n          <p className=\\"text-[20px] leading-[1.75] text-[#1a1a1a]\\">\\r\\n            When a stimulus gives reasons for a point, you are looking at argument structure. When it merely reports facts, you are not there yet.\\r\\n          </p>\\r\\n        </CalloutCard>\\r\\n      </LessonSection>\\r\\n\\r\\n      <LessonSection tocTitle=\\"Not every stimulus\\" md:grid-cols-2\\">\\r\\n          <GridCard title=\\"Mere information\\">\\r\\n            <p>The stimulus reports facts, events, or descriptions.</p>\\r\\n            <p>No sentence is being offered as a reason to accept a further claim.</p>\\r\\n          </GridCard>\\r\\n          <GridCard title=\\"Argument\\">\\r\\n            <p>At least one claim is doing support work for another claim.</p>\\r\\n            <p>The author is moving from reasons toward a point.</p>\\r\\n          </GridCard>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      </LessonSection>\\r\\n\\r\\n      <LessonSection tocTitle=\\"Premises and conclusions\\" className=\\"space-y-8\\">\\r\\n        <SectionHeading title=\\"Premises and conclusions\\" subtitle=\\"Support and claim\\" />\\r\\n        <div className=\\"grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2\\">\\r\\n          <GridCard title=\\"Premises ARE\\">\\r\\n            <RuleList items={[\\"Facts\\", \\"Evidence\\", \\"Reasons\\", \\"Support for another claim\\"]} />\\r\\n          </GridCard>\\r\\n          <GridCard title=\\"Premises ARE NOT\\">\\r\\n            <RuleList items={[\\"The author\'s final point\\", \\"The claim being proven\\", \\"The statement everything else points toward\\"]} />\\r\\n          </GridCard>\\r\\n          <GridCard title=\\"Conclusions ARE\\">\\r\\n            <RuleList items={[\\"The author\'s claim\\", \\"The point argued for\\", \\"The sentence supported by other claims\\"]} />\\r\\n          </GridCard>\\r\\n          <GridCard title=\\"Conclusions ARE NOT\\">\\r\\n            <RuleList items={[\\"Random background detail\\", \\"Automatically true just because it appears\\", \\"A sentence with no support relationship\\"]} />\\r\\n          </GridCard>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      </LessonSection>\\r\\n\\r\\n      <LessonSection tocTitle=\\"Anatomy example\\" className=\\"space-y-8\\">\\r\\n        <SectionHeading title=\\"Anatomy example\\" subtitle=\\"Label the parts of a simple argument\\" />\\r\\n        <CalloutCard className=\\"bg-[#FCFAF5]\\">\\r\\n          <p className=\\"font-serif text-[22px] italic leading-[1.7] text-[#1a1a1a]\\">\\r\\n            &quot;The city should extend library hours, because evening attendance has increased steadily for three years and nearby branches with longer hours serve more students after school.&quot;\\r\\n null) as Record<string, unknown> | null;\\r\\n          listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(row));\\r\\n        },\\r\\n      )\\r\\n      .subscribe();\\r\\n    watch = { channel, listeners };\\r\\n    profileWatches.set(userId, watch);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  watch.listeners.add(onChange);\\r\\n  return onChange;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction releaseProfileWatch(userId: string, listener: WatchListener) {\\r\\n  const watch = profileWatches.get(userId);\\r\\n  if (!watch) return;\\r\\n  watch.listeners.delete(listener);\\r\\n  if (watch.listeners.size === 0) {\\r\\n    profileWatches.delete(userId);\\r\\n    void supabase.removeChannel(watch.channel);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function useUserPermissions(): UserPermissions {\\r\\n  const { user, authReady, signOut } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n  const location = useLocation();\\r\\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\\r\\n  const userId = user?.id;\\r\\n\\r\\n  // The hook mounts once per ProtectedRoute, so the subscription is guarded by\\r\\n  // a single shared channel per user: the first mount opens it, the last unmount\\r\\n  // closes it. Without this, every gated page would hold its own socket.\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (!authReady || !userId) return;\\r\\n    const entry = acquireProfileWatch(userId, (row) => {\\r\\n      // Ignore self-caused / unrelated row updates (e.g. presence heartbeats\\r\\n      // writing last_seen_at). Only an actual access-flag change needs a\\r\\n      // refetch; otherwise every heartbeat would re-trigger the permissions\\r\\n      // query, which used to feed a fetch -> write -> event -> fetch loop.\\r\\n      if (row) {\\r\\n        const cached = queryClient.getQueryData<PermissionFlags>([\\"user-permissions\\", userId]);\\r\\n        if (cached) {\\r\\n          const archivedNow = \\"archived_at\\" in row && !!row.archived_at;\\r\\n          const changed = ACCESS_FLAGS.some(\\r\\n            (flag) => flag in row && !!row[flag] !== !!cached[flag],\\r\\n          );\\r\\n          if (!changed && !archivedNow) return;\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\\"user-permissions\\", userId] });\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    return () => releaseProfileWatch(userId, entry);\\r\\n  }, [authReady, userId, queryClient]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const query = useQuery<PermissionFlags>({\\r\\n    // Cached per user, shared across all ProtectedRoute / consumer mounts, so\\r\\n    // navigating between gated pages reads cached permissions synchronously\\r\\n    // instead of re-fetching (and re-showing the full-screen loading gate).\\r\\n    queryKey: [\\"user-permissions\\", userId],\\r\\n    enabled: authReady && !!user,\\r\\n    // Realtime (below) is the primary path for a revoke to land; the short\\r\\n    // stale window and the focus refetch are the fallback when the socket is\\r\\n    // down, so \\"Revoke All Access\\" is never more than a tab switch away from\\r\\n    // taking effect. [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.8 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '      return 70 * 60 * 1000;\r\n    case \'custom\':\r\n      return timer.customMinutes ? timer.customMinutes * 60 * 1000 : undefined;\r\n    default:\r\n      return undefined;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction DrillContent() {\r\n  const adaptiveEngine = useAdaptiveEngine();\r\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\r\n  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();\r\n  const { user } = useAuth();\r\n  // Depend on the id, never the user object: Supabase hands out a fresh object\r\n  // on every token refresh, and effects keyed on the object re-run for no reason.\r\n  const userId = user?.id;\r\n  // Teaching notes under the question are an admin surface. The panel stays\r\n  // open across questions once opened, so a teaching pass is one click, not ten.\r\n  const { is_admin: isAdmin } = useUserPermissions();\r\n  const [teachingNotesOpen, setTeachingNotesOpen] = React.useState(false);\r\n  const { settings } = useUserSettings();\r\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\r\n  const { isLoading: qbLoading } = useQuestionBank();\r\n\r\n  // Everything about the run itself comes from the Drill Run Spec, which the\r\n  // gate in front of this page built from the launch in the address bar. The\r\n  // runner does not parse the query string for it: one grammar, one reader.\r\n  const state = useDrillRunSpec();\r\n  const assignmentId = state.assignmentId ?? null;\r\n  const guidedAssignmentId = state.guidedAssignmentId ?? null;\r\n  const guidedDrillId = state.guidedDrillId ?? null;\r\n  const guidedSetId = state.guidedSetId;\r\n  const { set: guidedSet } = useHomeworkSet(guidedSetId);\r\n  const [guidedInfoOpen, setGuidedInfoOpen] = React.useState(false);\r\n\r\n  const practicingLabel = state.practicingLabel;\r\n\r\n  // Context params for header/breadcrumbs (survive refresh)\r\n  const origin = searchParams.get(\'origin\');\r\n  const returnTo = searchParams.get(\'returnTo\');\r\n  const drillReturnTo = state.returnTo ?? (returnTo && returnTo.startsWith(\'/\') && !returnTo.startsWith(\'//\') ? returnTo : undefined);\r\n  // Which run and leg this section belongs to, when it belongs to one. Comes\r\n  // from the launch rather than from returnTo: that string is a routing hint\r\n  // and nothing load-bearing may depend on it. A refreshed leg used to lose\r\n  // these and then had nothing to write its close-out against, so the section\r\n  // could never be marked finished and the practice test stayed open forever.\r\n  const ptRun = state.ptRun;\r\n  // Routing and score-UI suppression only. Never a clock or integrity decision.\r\n  const inSitting = isSittingReturn(drillReturnTo);\r\n  /**\r\n   * Answer and Explanation are not rendered at all for this run.\r\n   *\r\n   * Two runs are sealed. A practice-test sitting, because a sitting reveals\r\n   * nothing until it is scored and the old per-question gate quietly broke\r\n   * that: attempt a question inside a sitting and the reveal unlocked. And a\r\n   * run launched `?sealed=1`, which is how an assigned section is handed over.\r\n   *\r\n   * The full-section gate further down is a different and weaker thing. It\r\n   * withholds the reveal until the question is attempted and then allows it.\r\n   * That is right for a section a student chose to sit; it is wrong for one\r\n   * assigned as a measurement, where the answer to question 3 must not be\r\n   * available while question 4 is still open.\r\n   */\r\n  const revealSealed = inSitting || !!state.sealed;\r\n  const contextLabel = searchParams.get(\'label\');\r\n  const contextFocus = searchParams.get(\'focus\');\r\n  // Kept as a string: `?initialIndex=0` is a real request to open on the first\r\n  // question, and a number would read as absent.\r\n  const initialIndexParam = state.initialIndex != null ? String(state.initialIndex) : null;\r\n  const isMcPractice = state.practiceSource === \'intro-to-lr-practice-drills\';\r\n  // Maps the finished MC practice set back to its module-progress completion id\r\n  // so the Intro to LR path cannot report "core path cleared" before the final\r\n  // practice sets are actually done.\r\n  const mcPracticePool = React.useMemo(\r\n    () => (isMcPractice ? getPracticePool(state.practiceSlug ?? \'\') : undefined),\r\n    [isMcPractice, state.practiceSlug],\r\n  );\r\n  // Once the set is finished, snapshot writes must stop: a late write would\r\n  // flip the stored row back to in_progress and re-offer "Resume" on a set the\r\n  // student already completed.\r\n  const mcPracticeFinalizedRef = React.useRef(false);\r\n  // Guided drills keep the same kind of durable resume row, keyed by assignment.\r\n  // Null for every other drill surface, which is what leaves library, flagged,\r\n  // and Smart Drill runs untouched.\r\n  const guidedSessionKey = React.useMemo(\r\n    () => guidedSessionKeyForRunner({ guidedAssignmentId, guidedDrillId, returnTo }),\r\n    [guidedAssignmentId, guidedDrillId, returnTo],\r\n  );\r\n  const guidedLegacySessionKey = React.useMemo(\r\n    () =>\r\n      guidedDrillId && !guidedAssignmentId\r\n        ? guidedSessionKeyForRunner({ returnTo })\r\n        : null,\r\n    [guidedDrillId, guidedAssignmentId, returnTo],\r\n  );\r\n  // Same reason as mcPracticeFinalizedRef: once the drill is finished, a late\r\n  // snapshot write would flip the row back to in_progress and offer a resume on\r\n  // a drill the student already completed.\r\n  const guidedFinalizedRef = React.useRef(false);\r\n  const genericResumeIdentity = state.resumeIdentity;\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.8 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '  /**\r\n   * One day of a bootcamp, either its homework or its practice. The questions\r\n   * are picked fresh each time from that day\'s filters, so the day and the part\r\n   * are the run\'s real name — "Day 3 homework" — and the picked list is not.\r\n   * Naming it this way means the link survives a change to how the picker\r\n   * works, and the Run Record still holds the questions the student actually\r\n   * got.\r\n   *\r\n   * Its Run Record is keyed by the day and part. The old question-list key is\r\n   * read only as a migration fallback for runs started before that identity\r\n   * existed.\r\n   */\r\n  | { kind: \'bootcamp-day\'; day: number; part: \'homework\' | \'practice\' }\r\n  /**\r\n   * An ad-hoc list of specific questions: a question-finder jump, "redo this\r\n   * one", a generated micro-drill. It has no row to point at, so the list is\r\n   * the name.\r\n   */\r\n  | {\r\n      kind: \'questions\';\r\n      qids: string[];\r\n      /**\r\n       * Set when the list arrived from an older link or an in-memory handover\r\n       * that also named which stored row the run belongs to. Absent for a\r\n       * plain "drill these questions" link.\r\n       */\r\n      carry?: RunCarry;\r\n    }\r\n  /**\r\n   * "Six Flaw questions, medium." What the student asked for, rather than what\r\n   * it happened to draw. Refresh returns the same questions because the Run\r\n   * Record holds the queue; the filter only has to survive as the run\'s name.\r\n   */\r\n  | {\r\n      kind: \'filter\';\r\n      count: number;\r\n      qtypes?: string[];\r\n      difficulties?: number[];\r\n      pts?: number[];\r\n      sections?: number[];\r\n      qfeatures?: string[];\r\n    };\r\n\r\nexport type DrillLaunch = DrillLaunchBody & { options?: DrillLaunchOptions };\r\n\r\n/** The kinds whose question list lives in a row we have to read before starting. */\r\nconst KINDS_NEEDING_RESOLUTION = new Set([\r\n  \'saved-set\',\r\n  \'assignment\',\r\n  \'guided-drill\',\r\n  \'practice-set\',\r\n  \'bootcamp-day\',\r\n]);\r\n\r\nexport function launchNeedsResolution(launch: DrillLaunch): boolean {\r\n  return KINDS_NEEDING_RESOLUTION.has(launch.kind);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Thrown when a link asks for something that kind of drill cannot have. */\r\nexport class DrillLaunchError extends Error {\r\n  constructor(message: string) {\r\n    super(message);\r\n    this.name = \'DrillLaunchError\';\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n'}]

It never reuses a param that\r\n      // belongs to another kind, so a `questions` link cannot be mistaken for\r\n      // a section or a guided drill it merely remembers the id of.\r\n      const c = launch.carry;\r\n      if (c) {\r\n        if (c.mode !== 'type-drill') p.set('runMode', c.mode);\r\n        if (c.sealed) p.set('runSealed', '1');\r\n        if (c.isGuidedDrill) p.set('guided', '1');\r\n        if (c.guidedDrillId) p.set('guidedDrill', c.guidedDrillId);\r\n        if (c.guidedAssignmentId) p.set('guidedAssignment', c.guidedAssignmentId);\r\n        if (c.guidedDurationMinutes) p.set('guidedMinutes', String(c.guidedDurationMinutes));\r\n        if (c.assignmentId) p.set('assignment', c.assignmentId);\r\n        if (c.guidedSetId) p.set('guidedSet', c.guidedSetId);\r\n        if (c.practiceSource) p.set('source', c.practiceSource);\r\n        if (c.practiceSlug) p.set('slug', c.practiceSlug);\r\n        if (c.resumeIdentity) {\r\n          p.set('day', String(c.resumeIdentity.day));\r\n          p.set('part', c.resumeIdentity.part);\r\n        }\r\n        if (c.ptLeg) {\r\n          p.set('runId', c.ptLeg.runId);\r\n          p.set('leg', String(c.ptLeg.legIndex));\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n      break;\r\n    }\r\n    case 'filter':\r\n      p.set('count', String(launch.count));\r\n      setList(p, 'qtypes', launch.qtypes);\r\n      setList(p, 'difficulties', launch.difficulties);\r\n      setList(p, 'pts', launch.pts);\r\n      setList(p, 'sections', launch.sections);\r\n      setList(p, 'qfeatures', launch.qfeatures);\r\n      break;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  const o = launch.options;\r\n  if (o?.returnTo) p.set('returnTo', o.returnTo);\r\n  if (o?.label) p.set('label', o.label);\r\n  if (o?.initialIndex != null) p.set('initialIndex', String(o.initialIndex));\r\n  if (o?.present) p.set('present', o.present);\r\n\r\n"}]

Never needs to read anything: writing a\r\n * link is always possible from the launch alone, which is what lets `/drill`\r\n * canonicalize its own URL on arrival.\r\n *\r\n * Returns the query string without a leading `?`.\r\n */\r\nexport function launchDrill(launch: DrillLaunch): string {\r\n  const p = new URLSearchParams();\r\n  p.set('drill', launch.kind);\r\n\r\n  switch (launch.kind) {\r\n    case 'smart-drill':\r\n      break;\r\n    case 'section':\r\n      p.set('pt', String(launch.pt));\r\n      p.set('section', String(launch.section));\r\n      if (launch.timerMode) p.set('timer', launch.timerMode);\r\n      if (launch.minutes) p.set('minutes', String(launch.minutes));\r\n      if (launch.sealed) p.set('sealed', '1');\r\n      if (launch.ptLeg) {\r\n        p.set('runId', launch.ptLeg.runId);\r\n        p.set('leg', String(launch.ptLeg.legIndex));\r\n      }\r\n      break;\r\n    case 'saved-set':\r\n      p.set('set', launch.setId);\r\n      break;\r\n    case 'assignment':\r\n      p.set('assignment', launch.assignmentId);\r\n      if (launch.guidedSetId) p.set('guidedSet', launch.guidedSetId);\r\n      if (launch.timerMode) p.set('timer', launch.timerMode);\r\n      if (launch.revealAllowed) p.set('reveal', '1');\r\n      break;\r\n    case 'guided-drill':\r\n      if (launch.drillId) p.set('guidedDrill', launch.drillId);\r\n      if (launch.assignmentId) p.set('assignment', launch.assignmentId);\r\n      if (launch.durationMinutes) p.set('minutes', String(launch.durationMinutes));\r\n      break;\r\n    case 'practice-set':\r\n      p.set('slug', launch.slug);\r\n      if (launch.source) p.set('source', launch.source);\r\n      break;\r\n    case 'bootcamp-day':\r\n      p.set('day', String(launch.day));\r\n      p.set('part', launch.part);\r\n      break;\r\n    case 'questions': {\r\n"}]

If it is, separate the supporting reasons from the claim they are trying to prove.</p>\n+            </div>\n+            <div>\n+              <h3 className=\"text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground\">Worked example</h3>\n+              <blockquote className=\"mt-2 border-l-2 border-primary pl-4 font-serif text-base leading-relaxed text-foreground\">“The city should extend library hours, because evening attendance has increased steadily for three years and nearby branches with longer hours serve more students after school.”</blockquote>\n+              <dl className=\"mt-4 grid gap-3 text-sm sm:grid-cols-2\">\n+                <div className=\"rounded-lg bg-muted/50 p-3\"><dt className=\"font-semibold\">Conclusion</dt><dd className=\"mt-1\">The city should extend library hours.</dd></div>\n+                <div className=\"rounded-lg bg-muted/50 p-3\"><dt className=\"font-semibold\">Supporting reasons</dt><dd className=\"mt-1\">Evening attendance has increased; nearby branches with longer hours serve more students after school.</dd></div>\n+              </dl>\n+            </div>\n+            <p className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">From Intro to LR, Lesson 2: Argument Anatomy.</p>\n+          </div>\n+          <aside className=\"min-w-0 space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-border bg-background p-4\" aria-label=\"Main Conclusion next steps\">\n+            <div>\n+              <h3 className=\"font-semibold text-foreground\">Try the idea</h3>\n+              <p className=\"mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground\">Practice Main Conclusion questions, then review your first answers here.</p>\n+            </div>\n+            {canDrill ? [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 2.8 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';\r\nimport {\r\n  adaptLrAttempts,\r\n  adaptLrSections,\r\n  adaptRcAttempts,\r\n  completedPracticeTestIds,\r\n  projectFirstAnswerProgress,\r\n  type FirstAnswerProgress,\r\n  type GradedQuestionAnswer,\r\n  type LrAttemptSourceRow,\r\n  type LrSectionSourceRow,\r\n  type PracticeTestSourceRow,\r\n  type ProgressCoverage,\r\n  type ProgressWindow,\r\n  type RcAttemptSourceRow,\r\n  type RcSessionSourceRow,\r\n} from '@/lib/firstAnswerProgress';\r\nimport { assertActiveStudentScope } from '@/lib/studentScopedWork';\r\nimport type { StudentSubjectScope } from '@/lib/studentSubjectScope';\r\n\r\nconst PAGE_SIZE = 500;\r\n\r\nasync function allPages<T>(read: (from: number, to: number) => Promise<{ data: T[] | null; error: { message: string } | null }>): Promise<T[]> {\r\n  const rows: T[] = [];\r\n  for (let offset = 0; ; offset += PAGE_SIZE) {\r\n    const result = await read(offset, offset + PAGE_SIZE - 1);\r\n    if (result.error) throw new Error(result.error.message);\r\n    rows.push(...(result.data ?? []));\r\n    if ((result.data ?? []).length < PAGE_SIZE) return rows;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst readLrAttempts = (studentId: string) => allPages<LrAttemptSourceRow>(async (from, to) => {\r\n  const result = await supabase.from('attempts')\r\n    .select('id,user_id,qid,qtype,mode,correct,selected_answer,timestamp_iso')\r\n    .eq('user_id', studentId).order('id').range(from, to);\r\n  return result as unknown as { data: LrAttemptSourceRow[] | null; error: { message: string } | null };\r\n});\r\n\r\nconst readLrSections = (studentId: string) => allPages<LrSectionSourceRow>(async (from, to) => {\r\n  const result = await supabase.from('section_history')\r\n    .select('id,user_id,mode,completed_at,practice_test_run_id,questions_json')\r\n    .eq('user_id', studentId).order('id').range(from, to);\r\n  return result as unknown as { data: LrSectionSourceRow[] | null; error: { message: string } | null };\r\n});\r\n\r\nconst readRcAttempts = (studentId: string) => allPages<RcAttemptSourceRow>(async (from, to) => {\r\n  const result = await supabase.from('rc_attempts')\r\n    .select('id,user_id,session_id,passage_id,question_id,selected,is_correct,created_at')\r\n    .eq('user_id', studentId).order('id').range(from, to);\r\n  return result as unknown as { data: RcAttemptSourceRow[] | null; error: { message: string } | null };\r\n});\r\n\r\nconst readRcSessions = (studentId: string) => allPages<RcSessionSourceRow>(async (from, to) => {\r\n  const result = await supabase.from('rc_drill_sessions')\r\n    .select('id,user_id,status,completed_at,excluded_from_analytics,practice_test_run_id')\r\n    .eq('user_id', studentId).order('id').range(from, to);\r\n  return result as unknown as { data: RcSessionSourceRow[] | null; error: { message: string } | null };\r\n});\r\n\r\nconst readPracticeTests = (studentId: string) => allPages<PracticeTestSourceRow>(async (from, to) => {\r\n  const result = await supabase.from('practice_test_runs')\r\n    .select('id,user_id,status,completed_at')\r\n    .eq('user_id', studentId).order('id').range(from, to);\r\n  return result as unknown as { data: PracticeTestSourceRow[] | null; error: { message: string } | null };\r\n});\r\n\r\nconst errorReason = (reason: unknown) => reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : 'Source read failed';\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Read one student's persisted sources under their own session or an admin's\r\n * selected-student scope. Failed sources are named in coverage, never counted\r\n * as a genuine zero. The student and selected-student views share this ledger.\r\n */\r\nexport async function loadFirstAnswerLedger(scope: StudentSubjectScope): Promise<{\r\n  answers: GradedQuestionAnswer[];\r\n  coverage: ProgressCoverage;\r\n}> {\r\n  await assertActiveStudentScope(scope);\r\n  const studentId = scope.studentId;\r\n  const [lrAttemptsResult, lrSectionsResult, rcAttemptsResult, rcSessionsResult, runsResult] = await Promise.allSettled([\r\n    readLrAttempts(studentId),\r\n    readLrSections(studentId),\r\n    readRcAttempts(studentId),\r\n    readRcSessions(studentId),\r\n    readPracticeTests(studentId),\r\n  ]);\r\n\r\n  const coverage: ProgressCoverage = {\r\n    includedSources: [],\r\n    missingSources: [],\r\n    excludedRows: {},\r\n    knownLimitations: [\r\n      'Older section_history rows without user_id or per-question detail cannot be safely attributed to this student.',\r\n      'Unknown LR attempt modes and answers without a saved date are excluded.',\r\n      'Full PrepTest answers count only after the run is marked complete.',\r\n    ],\r\n  };\r\n  const runs = runsResult.status === 'fulfilled' ? runsResult.value : [];\r\n  if (runsResult.status === 'rejected') coverage.missingSources.push({ source: 'practice_test_runs', reason: errorReason(runsResult.reason) });\r\n"}]

Source adapters below deliberately exclude visits, blind review,\r\n * ungraded rows, and unfinished sections. Repeats remain in History elsewhere.\r\n */\r\nexport type ProgressArea = 'LR' | 'RC';\r\nexport type ProgressSource = 'lr_attempts' | 'lr_section_history' | 'rc_attempts';\r\nexport type ProgressWindow = 7 | 14 | 21 | 30 | 'all';\r\n\r\nexport interface GradedQuestionAnswer {\r\n  attemptId: string;\r\n  questionKey: string;\r\n  area: ProgressArea;\r\n  source: ProgressSource;\r\n  mode: string;\r\n  correct: boolean;\r\n  gradedAt: string;\r\n  /** Saved answer order when multiple choices become graded together at submission. */\r\n  answeredAt?: string;\r\n  /** Persisted LR question type. RC has no comparable topic in these rows. */\r\n  topic?: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface ProgressCoverage {\r\n  includedSources: ProgressSource[];\r\n  missingSources: Array<{ source: ProgressSource | 'rc_sessions' | 'practice_test_runs'; reason: string }>;\r\n  excludedRows: Record<string, number>;\r\n  knownLimitations?: string[];\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface AreaProgress {\r\n  correct: number;\r\n  denominator: number;\r\n  percent: number | null;\r\n  sample: 'insufficient' | 'early' | 'established';\r\n  evidenceAttemptIds: string[];\r\n  sources: ProgressSource[];\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface FirstAnswerProgress {\r\n  LR: AreaProgress;\r\n  RC: AreaProgress;\r\n  repeatCount: number;\r\n  coverage: ProgressCoverage;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface LrAttemptSourceRow {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  user_id: string | null;\r\n  qid: string;\r\n  mode: string;\r\n  correct: boolean;\r\n  selected_answer: string | null;\r\n  timestamp_iso: string | null;\r\n  qtype?: string | null;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface LrSectionSourceRow {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  user_id: string | null;\r\n  mode: string | null;\r\n  completed_at: string | null;\r\n  practice_test_run_id: string | null;\r\n  questions_json: unknown;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface RcAttemptSourceRow {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  user_id: string;\r\n  session_id: string;\r\n"}]

\"./StudentLibraryLessons\";\n+\n+const mocks = vi.hoisted(() => ({ permissions: vi.fn(), progress: vi.fn() }));\n+vi.mock(\"@/hooks/useUserPermissions\", () => ({ useUserPermissions: mocks.permissions }));\n+vi.mock(\"@/hooks/useFirstAnswerProgress\", () => ({ useFirstAnswerProgress: mocks.progress }));\n+\n+const answer = (id: string, key: string, correct: boolean, topic = \"Main Conclusion\") => ({\n+  attemptId: id, questionKey: key, area: \"LR\", source: \"lr_attempts\", mode: \"adaptive\", correct,\n+  gradedAt: `2026-09-2${id.slice(-1)}T12:00:00Z`, topic,\n+});\n+\n+describe(\"student written Library topic pilot\", () => {\n+  beforeEach(() => {\n+    mocks.permissions.mockReturnValue({\n+      loading: false, is_admin: false, has_drill_access: true, has_analytics_access: true,\n+      has_activity_access: true, has_bootcamp_access: false,\n+    });\n+    mocks.progress.mockReturnValue({\n+      ledger: {\n+        answers: [answer(\"a1\", \"q1\", true), answer(\"a2\", \"q1\", false), answer(\"a3\", \"q2\", false), answer(\"a4\", \"q3\", true, \"Flaw\")],\n+        coverage: { includedSources: [\"lr_attempts\"], missingSources: [] },\n+      },\n+      loading: false, error: null, resolution: { state: \"ready\" }, refresh: vi.fn(),\n+    });\n+  });\n+\n+  it(\"uses the existing written rule and worked example, then launches a Main Conclusion drill\", () => {\n+    render(<MemoryRouter><StudentLibraryLessons /></MemoryRouter>);\n+    expect(screen.getByText(/First decide whether the stimulus is an argument at Conclusion questions correct/)).toBeInTheDocument();\n+    const link = screen.getByRole(\"link\", { name: \"Practice this topic\" });\n+    const url = new URL(link.getAttribute(\"href\")!, \"https://lsatprep.study\");\n+    expect(url.pathname).toBe(\"/drill\");\n+    expect(url.searchParams.get(\"drill\")).toBe(\"filter\");\n+    expect(url.searchParams.get(\"qtypes\")).toBe(\"Main Conclusion\");\n+  });\n+\n+  it(\"does not fetch or expose topic evidence or drill entry without the relevant access\", () => {\n+    mocks.permissions.mockReturnValue({\n+      loading: false, is_admin: false, has_drill_access: false, has_analytics_access: false,\n+      has_activity_access: false, has_bootcamp_access: false,\n+    });\n+    render(<MemoryRouter><StudentLibraryLessons /></MemoryRouter>);\n+    expect(screen.queryByRole(\"link\", { name: \"Practice this topic\" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();\n+    expect(screen.getByText(\"Topic progress is locked for your
